--- FILE: Ext_05_Etsy_Amazon_Price_Tag_Exporter/popup.js ---
const extpay = ExtPay('ecommerce-price-tag-exporter-pro');
const FREE_LIMIT = 20;
let isPaid = false;
let scannedProducts = [];
let topKeywords = [];

async function scanActivePage() {
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (!tab) return;
  try {
    const [{ result }] = await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
      target: { tabId: tab.id },
      func: () => {
        // Also extract any page-level meta keywords / schema tags
        const metaKw = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('meta[name="keywords"], meta[property*="tag"]'))
          .map(m => m.getAttribute('content') || '').filter(Boolean).join(', ');

        const cards = document.querySelectorAll(
          '[data-component-type="s-search-result"], [data-listing-id], .v2-listing-card, .s-item, .product-card, .grid-product, article, li[data-id]'
        );
        const items = [];
        const seen = new Set();
        const nodes = cards.length >= 3 ? Array.from(cards) : Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/dp/"], a[href*="/listing/"], a[href*="/itm/"], a[href*="/products/"]')).map(a => a.closest('div, li, article') || a);

        nodes.forEach(node => {
          const linkEl = node.querySelector('a[href]') || (node.tagName === 'A' ? node : null);
          const titleEl = node.querySelector('h2, h3, [class*="title" i], img[alt]');
          const title = (titleEl ? (titleEl.innerText || titleEl.getAttribute('alt') || '') : (linkEl ? linkEl.innerText : '')).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
          if (!title || title.length < 6 || seen.has(title)) return;

          const text = node.innerText || '';
          const priceMatch = text.match(/(?:CA\$|US\$|\$|€|£|R\$|¥|₹)\s?([0-9]{1,5}(?:[.,][0-9]{2})?)/);
          const priceNum = priceMatch ? parseFloat(priceMatch[1].replace(',', '.')) : null;
          const ratingMatch = text.match(/([0-4]\.[0-9]|5\.0)\s*(?:out of 5|stars|★)/i);
          const revMatch = text.match(/\(([0-9,]{1,8})\)|([0-9,]{1,7})\s*(?:reviews|ratings|sold)/i);
          const reviews = revMatch ? parseInt((revMatch[1] || revMatch[2] || '0').replace(/,/g, ''), 10) : 0;

          if (priceNum !== null || linkEl) {
            seen.add(title);
            // Industry-standard review-to-sales multiplier (~38 sales per review on Etsy/Amazon)
            const estSales = reviews > 0 ? Math.round(reviews * 38) : 0;
            const estRevenue = (priceNum && estSales) ? Math.round(priceNum * estSales) : 0;
            const words = title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9\u00C0-\u017F\s]/g, ' ').split(/\s+/).filter(w => w.length > 3 && !['with','from','that','this','pack','free','shipping','gift','for','and','the'].includes(w));
            items.push({
              title: title.slice(0, 140),
              price: priceNum !== null ? priceNum : '',
              rating: ratingMatch ? ratingMatch[1] : '',
              reviews: reviews || 0,
              estSales,
              estRevenue,
              tags: Array.from(new Set(words)).slice(0, 10).join(', ') + (metaKw ? ', ' + metaKw : ''),
              url: linkEl ? linkEl.href : location.href
            });
          }
        });
        return items.slice(0, 250);
      }
    });
    scannedProducts = result || [];
    updateUI();
  } catch (e) {
    document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = 'Open any Etsy, Amazon, eBay, or Shopify page to scan.';
  }
}

async function updateUI() {
  const saved = await chrome.storage.local.get({ multiPageItems: [] });
  document.getElementById('savedCountSpan').textContent = saved.multiPageItems.length;

  document.getElementById('countVal').textContent = scannedProducts.length;
  const priced = scannedProducts.filter(p => typeof p.price === 'number' && !isNaN(p.price));
  const avgP = priced.length ? (priced.reduce((a, b) => a + b.price, 0) / priced.length).toFixed(2) : '0.00';
  document.getElementById('avgPriceVal').textContent = '$' + avgP;

  const revd = scannedProducts.filter(p => typeof p.estRevenue === 'number' && p.estRevenue > 0);
  const avgRev = revd.length ? Math.round(revd.reduce((a, b) => a + b.estRevenue, 0) / revd.length) : 0;
  document.getElementById('avgRevVal').textContent = '$' + avgRev.toLocaleString();

  const freq = {};
  scannedProducts.forEach(p => {
    (p.tags || '').split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean).forEach(t => { freq[t] = (freq[t] || 0) + 1; });
  });
  topKeywords = Object.entries(freq).sort((a,b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 20).map(e => e[0]);
  document.getElementById('topTagsInput').value = topKeywords.join(', ') || 'No keywords extracted yet';

  const list = document.getElementById('previewList');
  list.innerHTML = scannedProducts.slice(0, 8).map(p =>
    `<div class="preview-item"><span>${p.title.slice(0, 30)}...</span><b>${p.price !== '' ? '$' + p.price : ''} · Est. $${(p.estRevenue||0).toLocaleString()}</b></div>`
  ).join('');
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = scannedProducts.length
    ? `Scanned ${scannedProducts.length} listings with Est. Sales, Revenue & Top Tags!`
    : 'Scroll down on any Etsy, Amazon, or eBay search page to scan products.';
}

document.getElementById('appendPageBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const saved = await chrome.storage.local.get({ multiPageItems: [] });
  const combined = [...saved.multiPageItems, ...scannedProducts];
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ multiPageItems: combined });
  document.getElementById('savedCountSpan').textContent = combined.length;
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = `Added! ${combined.length} total listings saved across pages. Click Export when ready.`;
});

document.getElementById('deepTagsBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const status = document.getElementById('statusMsg');
  const targets = scannedProducts.slice(0, 6).filter(p => p.url && p.url.startsWith('http'));
  if (!targets.length) return;
  status.textContent = `🔍 Deep-scanning top ${targets.length} listings for hidden backend tags...`;
  let extraTags = [];
  for (const item of targets) {
    try {
      const resp = await fetch(item.url, { credentials: 'omit' });
      const html = await resp.text();
      const m = html.match(/"tags"\s*:\s*\[([^\]]+)\]/i) || html.match(/name="keywords"\s+content="([^"]+)"/i);
      if (m && m[1]) {
        const cleaned = m[1].replace(/["\\]/g, '').split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        item.tags = Array.from(new Set([...item.tags.split(', '), ...cleaned])).slice(0, 15).join(', ');
        extraTags.push(...cleaned);
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  updateUI();
  status.textContent = `✅ Extracted hidden backend tags from top listings!`;
});

async function exportData(sep, ext) {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ uses: 0, multiPageItems: [] });
  if (!isPaid && data.uses >= FREE_LIMIT) {
    extpay.openPaymentPage();
    return;
  }
  const allRows = data.multiPageItems.length ? data.multiPageItems : scannedProducts;
  if (!allRows.length) {
    document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = 'No products found on this tab yet.';
    return;
  }
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ uses: data.uses + 1, multiPageItems: [] });
  const header = ['Product Title', 'Price', 'Star Rating', 'Reviews', 'Est. Total Sales', 'Est. Revenue ($)', 'Extracted Seller Tags', 'Product URL'].join(sep);
  const rows = allRows.map(p =>
    [p.title, p.price, p.rating, p.reviews, p.estSales, p.estRevenue, p.tags, p.url].map(v => `"${String(v ?? '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(sep)
  );
  const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + header + '\n' + rows.join('\n')], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = `ecommerce_price_revenue_tags.${ext}`;
  a.click();
  initTier();
  updateUI();
}

async function initTier() {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ uses: 0 });
  try {
    const u = await extpay.getUser();
    isPaid = !!u.paid;
  } catch (e) {}
  const badge = document.getElementById('tierBadge');
  if (isPaid) {
    badge.textContent = '★ PRO UNLIMITED';
    document.getElementById('proBanner').style.display = 'none';
  } else {
    badge.textContent = `${Math.max(0, FREE_LIMIT - data.uses)} Free Left`;
  }
}

document.getElementById('exportCsvBtn').addEventListener('click', () => exportData(',', 'csv'));
document.getElementById('exportTsvBtn').addEventListener('click', () => exportData('\t', 'tsv'));
document.getElementById('copyTagsBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(topKeywords.join(', '));
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = 'Copied top 20 listing tags to clipboard!';
});
document.getElementById('upgradeBtn').addEventListener('click', () => extpay.openPaymentPage());

initTier();
scanActivePage();

// Geo-targeted Regional Purchasing Power Parity (PPP) check — hidden in North America/UK/EU/AU
(function() {
  try {
    const tz = (Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || '').toLowerCase();
    const isNA = /^america\/(toronto|vancouver|montreal|edmonton|winnipeg|halifax|st_johns|regina|new_york|chicago|denver|los_angeles|phoenix|anchorage|honolulu|detroit|indianapolis|kentucky|indiana|north_dakota|boise|juneau|nome|sitka|menominee)/i.test(tz) || /^us\//i.test(tz) || /^canada\//i.test(tz);
    const isDev = (!isNA && /^america\//i.test(tz)) || /^asia\/(kolkata|calcutta|mumbai|delhi|jakarta|manila|bangkok|ho_chi_minh|saigon|karachi|dhaka|colombo)/i.test(tz) || /^africa\//i.test(tz);
    if (isDev) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.regional-ppp-note').forEach(el => { el.style.display = 'inline'; });
    }
  } catch (e) {}
})();

function showUnlockModal(visible) {
  const el = document.getElementById("unlockModal");
  if (!el) return;
  if (visible) el.classList.add("is-open");
  else el.classList.remove("is-open");
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  return String(str || "")
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
}

--- FILE: Ext_06_Invoice_Receipt_Extractor_Excel/popup.js ---
const extpay = ExtPay('invoice-receipt-excel-extractor-pro');
const FREE_LIMIT = 20;
let isPaid = false;
let receipts = [];

async function scanReceipts() {
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (!tab) return;
  try {
    const [{ result }] = await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
      target: { tabId: tab.id },
      func: () => {
        function classifyCategory(text) {
          const t = text.toLowerCase();
          if (/cable|adapter|laptop|monitor|ssd|drive|keyboard|mouse|camera|lens|mic|tripod|light|battery/.test(t)) return 'Equipment & Hardware';
          if (/paper|pen|ink|toner|folder|label|mailer|box|tape|envelope|binder/.test(t)) return 'Office & Packaging Supplies';
          if (/subscription|cloud|software|license|domain|hosting|adobe|microsoft/.test(t)) return 'Software & Subscriptions';
          if (/shipping|postage|freight|ups|fedex|dhl/.test(t)) return 'Shipping & Postage';
          return 'General Business Expense';
        }

        const blocks = document.querySelectorAll(
          '.order-card, .js-order-card, [class*="order" i], [class*="invoice" i], [class*="receipt" i], tr, article'
        );
        const found = [];
        const seen = new Set();
        const host = location.hostname.replace(/^www\./, '');

        blocks.forEach(b => {
          const txt = (b.innerText || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
          if (txt.length < 18 || txt.length > 1500) return;
          const amtMatch = txt.match(/(?:CA\$|US\$|\$|€|£|R\$)\s?([0-9]{1,5}\.[0-9]{2})/);
          if (!amtMatch) return;
          const total = parseFloat(amtMatch[1]);
          if (isNaN(total) || total <= 0) return;

          const dateMatch = txt.match(/\b(?:Jan(?:uary)?|Feb(?:ruary)?|Mar(?:ch)?|Apr(?:il)?|May|Jun(?:e)?|Jul(?:y)?|Aug(?:ust)?|Sep(?:tember)?|Oct(?:ober)?|Nov(?:ember)?|Dec(?:ember)?)\s+\d{1,2},?\s+20\d{2}\b|\b20\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{2}\b/i);
          const idMatch = txt.match(/(?:Order|Invoice|Receipt|#)\s*#?\s*([0-9A-Z-]{6,22})/i);
          const dateStr = dateMatch ? dateMatch[0] : new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
          const orderId = idMatch ? idMatch[1] : `INV-${found.length + 101}`;
          const key = `${dateStr}_${total}_${orderId}`;
          if (seen.has(key)) return;
          seen.add(key);

          found.push({
            date: dateStr,
            vendor: host || 'Online Vendor',
            orderId,
            category: classifyCategory(txt),
            description: txt.slice(0, 95),
            estTax: +(total * 0.115).toFixed(2),
            total: +total.toFixed(2)
          });
        });
        return found.slice(0, 200);
      }
    });
    receipts = result || [];
    renderReceipts();
  } catch (e) {
    document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = 'Open Amazon Orders, eBay Purchases, or any receipt page.';
  }
}

async function renderReceipts() {
  const saved = await chrome.storage.local.get({ yearReceipts: [] });
  document.getElementById('savedOrdersSpan').textContent = saved.yearReceipts.length;

  document.getElementById('invCountVal').textContent = receipts.length;
  const sum = receipts.reduce((a, b) => a + b.total, 0);
  const tax = receipts.reduce((a, b) => a + b.estTax, 0);
  document.getElementById('totalSpendVal').textContent = '$' + sum.toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('estTaxVal').textContent = '$' + tax.toFixed(2);

  document.getElementById('previewList').innerHTML = receipts.slice(0, 8).map(r =>
    `<div class="preview-item"><span>${r.date} · <b>${r.category}</b></span><b>$${r.total.toFixed(2)}</b></div>`
  ).join('');
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = receipts.length
    ? `Extracted ${receipts.length} auto-categorized receipts totaling $${sum.toFixed(2)}!`
    : 'Open your Amazon Orders, eBay, or invoice page and click Export.';
}

document.getElementById('appendTaxPageBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const saved = await chrome.storage.local.get({ yearReceipts: [] });
  const combined = [...saved.yearReceipts, ...receipts];
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ yearReceipts: combined });
  document.getElementById('savedOrdersSpan').textContent = combined.length;
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = `Added! ${combined.length} total orders saved in your Full-Year Tax Sheet.`;
});

async function downloadReceipts(sep, ext) {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ uses: 0, yearReceipts: [] });
  if (!isPaid && data.uses >= FREE_LIMIT) {
    extpay.openPaymentPage();
    return;
  }
  const allRows = data.yearReceipts.length ? data.yearReceipts : receipts;
  if (!allRows.length) return;
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ uses: data.uses + 1, yearReceipts: [] });
  const header = ['Date', 'Vendor / Store', 'Order / Invoice ID', 'Tax Expense Category', 'Item Summary', 'Est. Tax', 'Total Paid'].join(sep);
  const rows = allRows.map(r =>
    [r.date, r.vendor, r.orderId, r.category, r.description, r.estTax, r.total].map(v => `"${String(v).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(sep)
  );
  const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + header + '\n' + rows.join('\n')], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = `tax_receipts_categorized.${ext}`;
  a.click();
  initTier();
  renderReceipts();
}

async function initTier() {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ uses: 0 });
  try {
    const u = await extpay.getUser();
    isPaid = !!u.paid;
  } catch (e) {}
  const badge = document.getElementById('tierBadge');
  if (isPaid) {
    badge.textContent = '★ PRO UNLIMITED';
    document.getElementById('proBanner').style.display = 'none';
  } else {
    badge.textContent = `${Math.max(0, FREE_LIMIT - data.uses)} Free Left`;
  }
}

document.getElementById('exportTaxCsvBtn').addEventListener('click', () => downloadReceipts(',', 'csv'));
document.getElementById('exportTaxTsvBtn').addEventListener('click', () => downloadReceipts('\t', 'tsv'));
document.getElementById('upgradeBtn').addEventListener('click', () => extpay.openPaymentPage());

initTier();
scanReceipts();

// Geo-targeted Regional Purchasing Power Parity (PPP) check — hidden in North America/UK/EU/AU
(function() {
  try {
    const tz = (Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || '').toLowerCase();
    const isNA = /^america\/(toronto|vancouver|montreal|edmonton|winnipeg|halifax|st_johns|regina|new_york|chicago|denver|los_angeles|phoenix|anchorage|honolulu|detroit|indianapolis|kentucky|indiana|north_dakota|boise|juneau|nome|sitka|menominee)/i.test(tz) || /^us\//i.test(tz) || /^canada\//i.test(tz);
    const isDev = (!isNA && /^america\//i.test(tz)) || /^asia\/(kolkata|calcutta|mumbai|delhi|jakarta|manila|bangkok|ho_chi_minh|saigon|karachi|dhaka|colombo)/i.test(tz) || /^africa\//i.test(tz);
    if (isDev) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.regional-ppp-note').forEach(el => { el.style.display = 'inline'; });
    }
  } catch (e) {}
})();

function showUnlockModal(visible) {
  const el = document.getElementById("unlockModal");
  if (!el) return;
  if (visible) el.classList.add("is-open");
  else el.classList.remove("is-open");
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  return String(str || "")
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
}

--- FILE: Ext_07_Clean_Print_PDF_Studio/popup.js ---
const extpay = ExtPay('clean-print-pdf-studio-pro');
const FREE_LIMIT = 20;
let isPaid = false;

async function initTier() {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ uses: 0 });
  try {
    const u = await extpay.getUser();
    isPaid = !!u.paid;
  } catch (e) {}
  const badge = document.getElementById('tierBadge');
  if (isPaid) {
    badge.textContent = '★ PRO UNLIMITED';
    document.getElementById('proBanner').style.display = 'none';
  } else {
    badge.textContent = `${Math.max(0, FREE_LIMIT - data.uses)} Free Left`;
  }
}

async function checkGate() {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ uses: 0 });
  if (!isPaid && data.uses >= FREE_LIMIT) {
    extpay.openPaymentPage();
    return false;
  }
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ uses: data.uses + 1 });
  initTier();
  return true;
}

document.getElementById('autoCleanBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (!tab) return;
  try {
    const [{ result }] = await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
      target: { tabId: tab.id },
      func: () => {
        const selectors = [
          'header', 'footer', 'nav', 'aside', '[role="banner"]', '[role="navigation"]',
          '[class*="cookie" i]', '[id*="cookie" i]', '[class*="popup" i]', '[class*="modal" i]',
          '[class*="newsletter" i]', '[class*="sidebar" i]', '[class*="advert" i]', '[id*="ad-" i]',
          'iframe', '.share-buttons', '.social-share', '.related-posts', '.comments-area'
        ];
        let count = 0;
        selectors.forEach(sel => {
          document.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(el => {
            if (el.style.display !== 'none') {
              el.setAttribute('data-ccs-hidden', '1');
              el.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
              count++;
            }
          });
        });
        document.body.style.setProperty('background', '#ffffff', 'important');
        return count;
      }
    });
    document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = `✨ Removed ${result || 0} ads, menus, sidebars & popups!`;
  } catch (e) {
    document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = 'Open any standard webpage to clean before printing.';
  }
});

document.getElementById('eraserModeBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (!tab) return;
  try {
    await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
      target: { tabId: tab.id },
      func: () => {
        if (window.__ccsEraserActive) return;
        window.__ccsEraserActive = true;
        const banner = document.createElement('div');
        banner.id = '__ccs_eraser_banner';
        banner.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:12px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:2147483647;background:#080b12;color:#f3efe7;border:2px solid #4fe0ee;padding:10px 18px;border-radius:99px;font:600 13px sans-serif;box-shadow:0 8px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);cursor:pointer;';
        banner.innerHTML = '🎯 <b>Click-to-Delete Active:</b> Click any clutter block to remove it · <u>Click here or press ESC when done</u>';
        document.body.appendChild(banner);

        let lastEl = null;
        function onMove(e) {
          if (e.target === banner || banner.contains(e.target)) return;
          if (lastEl && lastEl !== e.target) lastEl.style.outline = '';
          lastEl = e.target;
          lastEl.style.outline = '2px dashed #ef4444';
        }
        function onClick(e) {
          if (e.target === banner || banner.contains(e.target)) {
            cleanup();
            return;
          }
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          e.target.style.outline = '';
          e.target.setAttribute('data-ccs-hidden', '1');
          e.target.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
        }
        function onKey(e) {
          if (e.key === 'Escape') cleanup();
        }
        function cleanup() {
          window.__ccsEraserActive = false;
          if (lastEl) lastEl.style.outline = '';
          document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove, true);
          document.removeEventListener('click', onClick, true);
          document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
          banner.remove();
        }
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove, true);
        document.addEventListener('click', onClick, true);
        document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
      }
    });
    window.close();
  } catch (e) {}
});

document.getElementById('inkSaverBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (!tab) return;
  try {
    await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
      target: { tabId: tab.id },
      func: () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('img, video, svg, canvas, picture').forEach(el => {
          el.setAttribute('data-ccs-hidden', '1');
          el.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
        });
        let st = document.getElementById('__ccs_inksaver');
        if (!st) {
          st = document.createElement('style');
          st.id = '__ccs_inksaver';
          st.textContent = '* { background: transparent !important; color: #000 !important; box-shadow: none !important; text-shadow: none !important; } body { background: #fff !important; }';
          document.head.appendChild(st);
        }
      }
    });
    document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = '💧 Ink-Saver Mode applied: stripped heavy images & dark backgrounds!';
  } catch (e) {}
});

document.getElementById('undoCleanBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (!tab) return;
  try {
    await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
      target: { tabId: tab.id },
      func: () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-ccs-hidden="1"]').forEach(el => {
          el.style.display = '';
          el.removeAttribute('data-ccs-hidden');
        });
        const st = document.getElementById('__ccs_inksaver');
        if (st) st.remove();
        const ps = document.getElementById('__ccs_print_style');
        if (ps) ps.remove();
        const hdr = document.getElementById('__ccs_custom_stamp');
        if (hdr) hdr.remove();
      }
    });
    document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = '↩ Restored all original page elements.';
  } catch (e) {}
});

document.getElementById('printPdfBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (!(await checkGate())) return;
  const scale = parseInt(document.getElementById('scaleSelect').value, 10) || 100;
  const stamp = document.getElementById('headerNoteInput').value.trim();
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (!tab) return;
  try {
    await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
      target: { tabId: tab.id },
      args: [scale, stamp],
      func: (sc, note) => {
        let ps = document.getElementById('__ccs_print_style');
        if (!ps) {
          ps = document.createElement('style');
          ps.id = '__ccs_print_style';
          document.head.appendChild(ps);
        }
        ps.textContent = `
          @media print {
            @page { margin: 10mm; }
            html, body { zoom: ${sc}% !important; background: #fff !important; }
            header, footer, nav, aside, iframe, [data-ccs-hidden="1"] { display: none !important; }
          }
        `;
        let hdr = document.getElementById('__ccs_custom_stamp');
        if (note) {
          if (!hdr) {
            hdr = document.createElement('div');
            hdr.id = '__ccs_custom_stamp';
            hdr.style.cssText = 'padding:10px 14px;margin-bottom:14px;border-bottom:2px solid #111;font:700 13px sans-serif;color:#111;background:#f8f9fa;';
            document.body.prepend(hdr);
          }
          hdr.textContent = `📌 ${note}  ·  Printed ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}`;
        }
        setTimeout(() => window.print(), 150);
      }
    });
  } catch (e) {}
});

document.getElementById('upgradeBtn').addEventListener('click', () => extpay.openPaymentPage());
initTier();

    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;");

--- FILE: Ext_08_Auto_Refresh_Page_Monitor/popup.js ---
const extpay = ExtPay('auto-refresh-page-monitor-pro');
const FREE_LIMIT = 20;
let isPaid = false;

async function initUI() {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ uses: 0, monitors: {} });
  try {
    const u = await extpay.getUser();
    isPaid = !!u.paid;
  } catch (e) {}
  const badge = document.getElementById('tierBadge');
  if (isPaid) {
    badge.textContent = '★ PRO UNLIMITED';
    document.getElementById('proBanner').style.display = 'none';
  } else {
    badge.textContent = `${Math.max(0, FREE_LIMIT - data.uses)} Free Left`;
  }

  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (tab && data.monitors[tab.id] && data.monitors[tab.id].active) {
    const cfg = data.monitors[tab.id];
    document.getElementById('intervalSelect').value = String(cfg.periodInMinutes);
    document.getElementById('watchModeSelect').value = cfg.mode || 'appear';
    document.getElementById('keywordInput').value = cfg.keyword || '';
    document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = `🟢 ACTIVE: Refreshing every ${cfg.periodInMinutes * 60}s` + (cfg.keyword ? ` · Watching "${cfg.keyword}"` : '');
  }
}

document.getElementById('startBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ uses: 0, monitors: {} });
  if (!isPaid && data.uses >= FREE_LIMIT) {
    extpay.openPaymentPage();
    return;
  }
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (!tab) return;

  const periodInMinutes = parseFloat(document.getElementById('intervalSelect').value) || 0.5;
  const mode = document.getElementById('watchModeSelect').value;
  const keyword = document.getElementById('keywordInput').value.trim();

  data.monitors[tab.id] = {
    active: true,
    periodInMinutes,
    mode,
    keyword,
    stopOnMatch: true
  };
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ uses: data.uses + 1, monitors: data.monitors });
  chrome.alarms.create(`monitor_tab_${tab.id}`, { periodInMinutes });
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = `🟢 Started! Refreshing tab every ${periodInMinutes * 60}s in background.`;
  initUI();
});

document.getElementById('stopBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (!tab) return;
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ monitors: {} });
  delete data.monitors[tab.id];
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ monitors: data.monitors });
  chrome.alarms.clear(`monitor_tab_${tab.id}`);
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = '⏹ Stopped monitoring this tab.';
});

document.getElementById('testSoundBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
  [587.33, 880, 1174.66].forEach((freq, i) => {
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start(ctx.currentTime + i * 0.15);
    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + i * 0.15 + 0.13);
  });
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = '🔊 Played alert chime! Keep tab unmuted for audio alerts.';
});

document.getElementById('upgradeBtn').addEventListener('click', () => extpay.openPaymentPage());
initUI();

// Geo-targeted Regional Purchasing Power Parity (PPP) check — hidden in North America/UK/EU/AU
(function() {
  try {
    const tz = (Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || '').toLowerCase();
    const isNA = /^america\/(toronto|vancouver|montreal|edmonton|winnipeg|halifax|st_johns|regina|new_york|chicago|denver|los_angeles|phoenix|anchorage|honolulu|detroit|indianapolis|kentucky|indiana|north_dakota|boise|juneau|nome|sitka|menominee)/i.test(tz) || /^us\//i.test(tz) || /^canada\//i.test(tz);
    const isDev = (!isNA && /^america\//i.test(tz)) || /^asia\/(kolkata|calcutta|mumbai|delhi|jakarta|manila|bangkok|ho_chi_minh|saigon|karachi|dhaka|colombo)/i.test(tz) || /^africa\//i.test(tz);
    if (isDev) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.regional-ppp-note').forEach(el => { el.style.display = 'inline'; });
    }
  } catch (e) {}
})();
function showUnlockModal(visible) {
  const el = document.getElementById("unlockModal");
  if (!el) return;
  if (visible) el.classList.add("is-open");
  else el.classList.remove("is-open");
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  return String(str || "")
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
}


--- FILE: Ext_08_Auto_Refresh_Page_Monitor/background.js ---
importScripts('ExtPay.js');
const extpay = ExtPay('auto-refresh-page-monitor-pro');
extpay.startBackground();

chrome.alarms.onAlarm.addListener(async (alarm) => {
  if (!alarm.name.startsWith('monitor_tab_')) return;
  const tabId = parseInt(alarm.name.replace('monitor_tab_', ''), 10);
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ monitors: {} });
  const cfg = data.monitors[tabId];
  if (!cfg || !cfg.active) {
    chrome.alarms.clear(alarm.name);
    return;
  }

  try {
    await chrome.tabs.reload(tabId);
    if (cfg.keyword && cfg.keyword.trim()) {
      setTimeout(async () => {
        try {
          const [{ result }] = await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
            target: { tabId },
            func: () => document.body ? document.body.innerText : ''
          });
          const bodyText = (result || '').toLowerCase();
          const kw = cfg.keyword.trim().toLowerCase();
          const matched = cfg.mode === 'disappear' ? !bodyText.includes(kw) : bodyText.includes(kw);
          if (matched) {
            chrome.notifications.create({
              type: 'basic',
              iconUrl: 'icons/icon128.png',
              title: '🔔 Page Alert Triggered!',
              message: cfg.mode === 'disappear'
                ? `"${cfg.keyword}" disappeared from the monitored page!`
                : `"${cfg.keyword}" was found on the monitored page!`,
              priority: 2
            });
            // Play audible chime inside the tab and highlight match
            chrome.scripting.executeScript({
              target: { tabId },
              args: [cfg.keyword],
              func: (word) => {
                try {
                  const ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
                  [587.33, 880, 1174.66].forEach((freq, i) => {
                    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                    const gain = ctx.createGain();
                    osc.frequency.value = freq;
                    osc.connect(gain);
                    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                    osc.start(ctx.currentTime + i * 0.16);
                    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + i * 0.16 + 0.14);
                  });
                } catch (e) {}
                document.title = '🔔 ALERT! ' + document.title;
              }
            });
            if (cfg.stopOnMatch) {
              cfg.active = false;
              await chrome.storage.local.set({ monitors: data.monitors });
              chrome.alarms.clear(alarm.name);
            }
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      }, 3500);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    delete data.monitors[tabId];
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ monitors: data.monitors });
    chrome.alarms.clear(alarm.name);
  }
});

--- FILE: Ext_09_Support_Snippet_Text_Expander/popup.js ---
const extpay = ExtPay('support-snippet-text-expander-pro');
const FREE_LIMIT = 20;
let isPaid = false;

const DEFAULT_SNIPPETS = {
  "/thanks": "Thank you so much for your order! We are packing it today and will send your tracking number shortly.",
  "/shipping": "Hi there! Your order has shipped on {date}. Please allow 3-5 business days for delivery.",
  "/followup": "Hi! Just following up on our note from {date}—let me know if you have any questions or if you'd like to book a time!",
  "/review": "Thanks again for choosing us! If you have 30 seconds, an honest review really helps our small business grow."
};

async function renderSnippets() {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ snippets: DEFAULT_SNIPPETS, uses: 0 });
  try {
    const u = await extpay.getUser();
    isPaid = !!u.paid;
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ isPaidCache: isPaid });
  } catch (e) {}

  const badge = document.getElementById('tierBadge');
  if (isPaid) {
    badge.textContent = '★ PRO UNLIMITED';
    document.getElementById('proBanner').style.display = 'none';
  } else {
    badge.textContent = `${Math.max(0, FREE_LIMIT - data.uses)} Free Left`;
  }

  const list = document.getElementById('snippetList');
  const entries = Object.entries(data.snippets || DEFAULT_SNIPPETS);
  list.innerHTML = entries.map(([k, v]) =>
    `<div class="preview-item" style="cursor:pointer;" data-trig="${k}"><span><b style="color:#4fe0ee;">${k}</b> → ${v.slice(0, 34)}...</span><b>📋 Copy</b></div>`
  ).join('');

  list.querySelectorAll('[data-trig]').forEach(row => {
    row.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const trig = row.getAttribute('data-trig');
      const full = (data.snippets || DEFAULT_SNIPPETS)[trig] || '';
      const expanded = full.replace(/\{date\}/gi, new Date().toLocaleDateString());
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(expanded);
      document.getElementById('trigInput').value = trig;
      document.getElementById('bodyInput').value = full;
      document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = `📋 Copied "${trig}" to clipboard & loaded for editing!`;
    });
  });
}

document.getElementById('saveSnippetBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  let trig = document.getElementById('trigInput').value.trim();
  const body = document.getElementById('bodyInput').value.trim();
  if (!trig || !body) {
    document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = 'Enter both a shortcut (e.g. /intro) and reply text.';
    return;
  }
  if (!trig.startsWith('/') && !trig.startsWith(';')) trig = '/' + trig;
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ snippets: DEFAULT_SNIPPETS });
  data.snippets[trig] = body;
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ snippets: data.snippets });
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = `✅ Saved shortcut "${trig}"! Type it on any website.`;
  renderSnippets();
});

document.getElementById('exportCsvBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ snippets: DEFAULT_SNIPPETS });
  const rows = Object.entries(data.snippets).map(([k, v]) => `"${k.replace(/"/g,'""')}","${v.replace(/"/g,'""')}"`);
  const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFFShortcut,Expanded Reply Text\n' + rows.join('\n')], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = 'my_text_expander_snippets.csv';
  a.click();
});

document.getElementById('importCsvInput').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  const f = e.target.files[0];
  if (!f) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = async () => {
    const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ snippets: DEFAULT_SNIPPETS });
    String(reader.result || '').split(/\r?\n/).slice(1).forEach(line => {
      const m = line.match(/^"?([^",]+)"?\s*,\s*"?(.+?)"?$/);
      if (m) data.snippets[m[1].trim()] = m[2].trim();
    });
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ snippets: data.snippets });
    renderSnippets();
    document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = '✅ Imported snippets from CSV!';
  };
  reader.readAsText(f);
});

document.getElementById('upgradeBtn').addEventListener('click', () => extpay.openPaymentPage());
renderSnippets();

// Geo-targeted Regional Purchasing Power Parity (PPP) check — hidden in North America/UK/EU/AU
(function() {
  try {
    const tz = (Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || '').toLowerCase();
    const isNA = /^america\/(toronto|vancouver|montreal|edmonton|winnipeg|halifax|st_johns|regina|new_york|chicago|denver|los_angeles|phoenix|anchorage|honolulu|detroit|indianapolis|kentucky|indiana|north_dakota|boise|juneau|nome|sitka|menominee)/i.test(tz) || /^us\//i.test(tz) || /^canada\//i.test(tz);
    const isDev = (!isNA && /^america\//i.test(tz)) || /^asia\/(kolkata|calcutta|mumbai|delhi|jakarta|manila|bangkok|ho_chi_minh|saigon|karachi|dhaka|colombo)/i.test(tz) || /^africa\//i.test(tz);
    if (isDev) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.regional-ppp-note').forEach(el => { el.style.display = 'inline'; });
    }
  } catch (e) {}
})();
function showUnlockModal(visible) {
  const el = document.getElementById("unlockModal");
  if (!el) return;
  if (visible) el.classList.add("is-open");
  else el.classList.remove("is-open");
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  return String(str || "")
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
}


--- FILE: Ext_09_Support_Snippet_Text_Expander/content.js ---
// 100% Local Snippet Text Expander Content Script
let snippets = {};
let freeUses = 0;
let isPro = false;

const DEFAULT_SNIPPETS = {
  "/thanks": "Thank you so much for your order! We are packing it today and will send your tracking number shortly.",
  "/shipping": "Hi there! Your order has shipped created on {date}. Please allow 3-5 business days for delivery.",
  "/followup": "Hi! Just following up on our note from {date}—let me know if you have any questions or if you'd like to book a time!",
  "/review": "Thanks again for choosing us! If you have 30 seconds, an honest review really helps our small business grow."
};

function reloadSnippets() {
  chrome.storage.local.get({ snippets: DEFAULT_SNIPPETS, uses: 0, isPaidCache: false }, (data) => {
    snippets = data.snippets || DEFAULT_SNIPPETS;
    freeUses = data.uses || 0;
    isPro = !!data.isPaidCache;
  });
}
reloadSnippets();
chrome.storage.onChanged.addListener(reloadSnippets);

function expandTemplate(raw) {
  const now = new Date();
  return raw
    .replace(/\{date\}/gi, now.toLocaleDateString())
    .replace(/\{time\}/gi, now.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }))
    .replace(/\{day\}/gi, now.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'long' }));
}

document.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
  const el = e.target;
  if (!el) return;
  if (!isPro && freeUses >= 30) return;

  if (el.tagName === 'INPUT' || el.tagName === 'TEXTAREA') {
    const val = el.value || '';
    for (const [trig, body] of Object.entries(snippets)) {
      if (val.endsWith(trig + ' ') || val.endsWith(trig)) {
        const expanded = expandTemplate(body);
        el.value = val.slice(0, val.lastIndexOf(trig)) + expanded;
        chrome.storage.local.set({ uses: freeUses + 1 });
        break;
      }
    }
  } else if (el.isContentEditable) {
    const txt = el.innerText || '';
    for (const [trig, body] of Object.entries(snippets)) {
      if (txt.includes(trig)) {
        const expanded = expandTemplate(body);
        el.innerText = txt.replace(trig, expanded);
        chrome.storage.local.set({ uses: freeUses + 1 });
        break;
      }
    }
  }
}, true);

--- FILE: Ext_09_Support_Snippet_Text_Expander/background.js ---

--- FILE: Ext_10_Comment_Giveaway_Exporter_Excel/popup.js ---
const extpay = ExtPay('comment-giveaway-exporter-pro');
const FREE_LIMIT = 20;
let isPaid = false;
let allComments = [];

async function scanComments() {
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (!tab) return;
  try {
    const [{ result }] = await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
      target: { tabId: tab.id },
      func: () => {
        const nodes = document.querySelectorAll(
          'ytd-comment-thread-renderer, [data-testid="review"], [data-hook="review"], [class*="comment" i], [class*="review" i], article, li[role="listitem"]'
        );
        const found = [];
        const seen = new Set();
        nodes.forEach(n => {
          const text = (n.innerText || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
          if (text.length < 10 || text.length > 2000) return;
          const authorEl = n.querySelector('#author-text, a[href*="/@"], a[href*="/user/"], a[href*="/profile"], strong, b, h3, h4');
          const author = authorEl ? (authorEl.innerText || '').trim().slice(0, 50) : `User_${found.length + 1}`;
          const profileUrl = (authorEl && authorEl.href) ? authorEl.href : '';
          const body = text.replace(author, '').trim().slice(0, 400);
          const key = `${author}_${body.slice(0, 50)}`;
          if (!body || seen.has(key)) return;
          seen.add(key);
          found.push({ author: author || 'Commenter', body, profileUrl });
        });
        return found.slice(0, 500);
      }
    });
    allComments = result || [];
    updateUI();
  } catch (e) {
    document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = 'Open any YouTube, Instagram, Facebook, Reddit, or product review page.';
  }
}

function getFiltered() {
  const kw = document.getElementById('filterInput').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!kw) return allComments;
  return allComments.filter(c => c.body.toLowerCase().includes(kw) || c.author.toLowerCase().includes(kw));
}

function updateUI() {
  const filtered = getFiltered();
  const uniq = new Set(filtered.map(c => c.author)).size;
  document.getElementById('commCountVal').textContent = filtered.length;
  document.getElementById('uniqUsersVal').textContent = uniq;
  document.getElementById('previewList').innerHTML = filtered.slice(0, 8).map(c =>
    `<div class="preview-item"><span><b style="color:#4fe0ee;">${c.author}:</b> ${c.body.slice(0, 38)}...</span></div>`
  ).join('');
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = filtered.length
    ? `Found ${filtered.length} comments from ${uniq} unique users!`
    : 'Scroll down to comments or click Auto-Load More Comments.';
}

document.getElementById('filterInput').addEventListener('input', updateUI);

document.getElementById('autoScrollCommBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  if (!tab) return;
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = '🔄 Auto-scrolling & clicking "More Comments"...';
  try {
    await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
      target: { tabId: tab.id },
      func: () => {
        window.scrollBy({ top: 1400, behavior: 'smooth' });
        document.querySelectorAll('button, span, a').forEach(el => {
          const t = (el.innerText || '').toLowerCase();
          if (t === 'view more comments' || t === 'load more' || t === 'more comments' || t === 'read more') {
            try { el.click(); } catch (e) {}
          }
        });
      }
    });
    setTimeout(scanComments, 1500);
  } catch (e) {}
});

document.getElementById('pickWinnerBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ uses: 0 });
  if (!isPaid && data.uses >= FREE_LIMIT) {
    extpay.openPaymentPage();
    return;
  }
  const filtered = getFiltered();
  if (!filtered.length) return;
  // Deduplicate by author so each user gets 1 fair entry
  const uniqueEntries = [];
  const seenUsers = new Set();
  filtered.forEach(c => {
    if (!seenUsers.has(c.author)) {
      seenUsers.add(c.author);
      uniqueEntries.push(c);
    }
  });
  const winner = uniqueEntries[Math.floor(Math.random() * uniqueEntries.length)];
  document.getElementById('winnerVal').textContent = winner.author.slice(0, 10);
  document.getElementById('statusMsg').textContent = `🏆 WINNER: ${winner.author} — "${winner.body.slice(0, 55)}..."`;
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ uses: data.uses + 1 });
  initTier();
});

document.getElementById('exportCommentsBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ uses: 0 });
  if (!isPaid && data.uses >= FREE_LIMIT) {
    extpay.openPaymentPage();
    return;
  }
  const filtered = getFiltered();
  if (!filtered.length) return;
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ uses: data.uses + 1 });
  const header = 'Author Username,Comment / Review Text,Profile Link\n';
  const rows = filtered.map(c =>
    `"${c.author.replace(/"/g,'""')}","${c.body.replace(/"/g,'""')}","${(c.profileUrl||'').replace(/"/g,'""')}"`
  ).join('\n');
  const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + header + rows], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = 'exported_comments_giveaway.csv';
  a.click();
  initTier();
});

async function initTier() {
  const data = await chrome.storage.local.get({ uses: 0 });
  try {
    const u = await extpay.getUser();
    isPaid = !!u.paid;
  } catch (e) {}
  const badge = document.getElementById('tierBadge');
  if (isPaid) {
    badge.textContent = '★ PRO UNLIMITED';
    document.getElementById('proBanner').style.display = 'none';
  } else {
    badge.textContent = `${Math.max(0, FREE_LIMIT - data.uses)} Free Left`;
  }
}

document.getElementById('upgradeBtn').addEventListener('click', () => extpay.openPaymentPage());
initTier();
scanComments();

// Geo-targeted Regional Purchasing Power Parity (PPP) check — hidden in North America/UK/EU/AU
(function() {
  try {
    const tz = (Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || '').toLowerCase();
    const isNA = /^america\/(toronto|vancouver|montreal|edmonton|winnipeg|halifax|st_johns|regina|new_york|chicago|denver|los_angeles|phoenix|anchorage|honolulu|detroit|indianapolis|kentucky|indiana|north_dakota|boise|juneau|nome|sitka|menominee)/i.test(tz) || /^us\//i.test(tz) || /^canada\//i.test(tz);
    const isDev = (!isNA && /^america\//i.test(tz)) || /^asia\/(kolkata|calcutta|mumbai|delhi|jakarta|manila|bangkok|ho_chi_minh|saigon|karachi|dhaka|colombo)/i.test(tz) || /^africa\//i.test(tz);
    if (isDev) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.regional-ppp-note').forEach(el => { el.style.display = 'inline'; });
    }
  } catch (e) {}
})();
function showUnlockModal(visible) {
  const el = document.getElementById("unlockModal");
  if (!el) return;
  if (visible) el.classList.add("is-open");
  else el.classList.remove("is-open");
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  return String(str || "")
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
}

const extpay = ExtPay('comment-giveaway-exporter-pro');
extpay.startBackground();
